## 6) V1.2 Required Contract
Required output for V1.2:

scenario_levels:
  activation:
    price: number|null
    label_ar: "مستوى التفعيل"
    source: "computed|unavailable"
  arrival:
    price: number|null
    label_ar: "مستوى الوصول"
    source: "computed|unavailable"
  review:
    price: number|null
    label_ar: "مستوى المراجعة"
    source: "computed|unavailable"
  invalidation:
    price: number|null
    label_ar: "مستوى الإلغاء"
    source: "computed|unavailable"

Rules:
- Do not fake levels.
- If computation is unavailable, return null with source=unavailable.
- Frontend must show unavailable, not invented numbers.
- Keep existing V1 fields unchanged.
